# Paper v8 independent content-delta audit

Reviewer did not author the supplied draft. V8 was compared by sentence content with the committed v7 source; the inventory below includes unchanged/moved material as a conservative superset, every table cell, and every caption. No paper edits, new scientific runs, model evaluation or truth access occurred. Todd-licensed deviations: title; opening framing; forecast-sign shorthand defined at first use; labelled post hoc/development framing. Citation descriptions are outside receipt scope and author-verified against source.

Numeric candidates below are scoped to cited receipt branches and retain full precision; the verdict additionally checks model, lead, amplitude, aggregation, bounds and direction. Integer question/pair/draw counts and equation/section/table labels are structural. Percent brackets omit repeated percent signs.

| V8_239_01 / 239 | For each instance, action and lead we compute the posterior correlation $\rho_k$ between $J_k$ and $J_8$, the share of the summed variance left in the difference, $c_k = \operatorname{Var}(D_k)/[\operatorname{Var}(J_k) + \operatorname{Var}(J_8)] = 1 - 2\operatorname{Cov}(J_k, J_8)/[\operatorname{Var}(J_k) + \operatorname{Var}(J_8)]$, and two signal-to-noise ratios, $z_D = \|\mathbb E D_k\| / \operatorname{sd}(D_k)$ for the intervention and $z_F = \|\mathbb E J_8 - \Jbar\| / \operatorname{sd}(J_8)$ for the forecast anomaly. ## Independent semantic checks

The inequality in Section 4.2 is valid. Let v0 and vk be the two positive marginal variances: 1-c = rho times 2 sqrt(v0 vk)/(v0+vk). If c<1 then covariance and rho are positive; the multiplying factor is at most one by the arithmetic-geometric mean inequality. Hence rho >= 1-c. This imposes no distribution-free conversion from z to posterior sign probability.

The Taylor proof uses the stated square-integrable second-derivative bound and nonzero variances; Cauchy-Schwarz controls the covariance remainders. The no-zero-atom condition supports sign-probability convergence. Cyclic equivariance plus a shift-invariant input law and linear dependence on the pattern gives zero climatological tangent mean for zero-mean patterns. The finite null ensemble is a descriptive check, not a proof of invariant-measure uniqueness or stationarity.

Tangent verification is eight draws from case zero for JAX JVP, every pattern and lead; the central-difference check is four draws of case zero and all eight patterns through 3 LT. Production tangent readings use every saved scoring draw. The manuscript states both restricted validation sample sizes, not validation of every case.

The Stage 9 variance receipt confirms median injection share exceeds median mean-flow share only at lead zero in both groups. Covariance terms are retained; medians of shares need not sum to unity. The mean-flow identity follows by summing lag-two minus lag-one products; global energy cancellation does not apply to the block boundaries. Mechanism associations remain descriptive.

Stage 13 decisions: each Table 9 cell was checked at its row model/lead/policy; capture uses improvement divided by no-action regret. Harm tests compare paired indicators, and bootstrap intervals are descriptive. No invalid case appears in these model receipts. Figure 11 uses the same mean regret and printed harm counts. Withheld-effect summaries compare all-eight confident versus not-confident questions, not seven-pattern or observation-confident cohorts. Full-grid changes distinguish draw answers, confidence labels and realized answers.

Worked-instance selection follows the modal earlier-loss combination and then lowest case/action indices, without outcome-based selection; F16 matches saved quantiles, modal probabilities and distinct truth markers. F15 uses Stage 6 share series and Stage 9 case-averaged first-loss bars. All figure files exist; every reference target is defined once. Target numbering is recorded below.

CNN-F is a descriptive positive control, with different training data/noise/forcing coverage from CNN-20k; the limitation paragraph identifies this confounding and no equivalence claim is inferred. Table 7 and appendix Table tab:resp use case-averaged confident-error rates, separate one-sided bounds and fixed posterior eligible cohorts. Pooled percentages in the abstract are different quantities.

Terminal probe: the prior independent audit (ACD_PAPER_AUDIT_v6.md, terminal probe identity) established that the evaluated response-model selected step is the terminal scheduled step. acd_stage9_train.py saves one checkpoint dictionary to the scheduled and selected archives without an intervening optimizer update. The d90baff… versus db12953… archive hash difference reflects distinct archive stems, not established differing weights. This is a retained code/provenance check; Stage 10 artifacts and Spark were not reopened. Loss ratio and weighted fraction are read through the existing registry, not recalculated or retuned here: ACD_POSTHOC_STAGE10_TERMINAL_BASELINE_DIFFERENCE_TO_BASE_RATIO = 33.75250032517367; ACD_POSTHOC_STAGE10_TERMINAL_BASELINE_WEIGHTED_DIFFERENCE_FRACTION = 0.9712250919892624.

## FIX items

- V8_122_02 (line 122): The seven-pattern curve is post hoc, but the caption labels only the pre-registered right-hand reading. Proposed replacement: Left: confident shares on the confirmation panel; the seven-pattern comparison is post hoc. Forecast sign: solid circles; all eight intervention signs: dashed squares; seven patterns: dash-dot triangles; observation-confident signs: dotted diamonds.
- V8_224_05 (line 224): The rounded maximum is smaller than the actual maximum; a strict upper bound cannot be stated at that rounded value. Proposed replacement: The largest share of draw-level intervention-sign answers that change is 0.0011 (rounded), at 6 LT.
- V8_288_04 (line 288): Mean divided by standard deviation does not determine sign probability for a general posterior; the next sentence correctly invokes the full tangent distribution. Proposed replacement: It does not establish how much posterior probability lies on either side of zero; the mean relative to its own spread is a descriptive summary, not a determination of sign confidence.
- V8_366_07 (line 366): The direct cost-head emulator was evaluated only at one lead and has no paired endpoint in the Stage 9 receipt; the universal statement includes an untested model. Proposed replacement: Every rollout emulator with a paired endpoint reproduces the paired ordering; the direct cost head has no paired endpoint in this evaluation.
- V8_513_01 (line 513): Moving this material into the appendix leaves the training/result paragraph without a local post hoc label; the later table and figure captions do not label the paragraph itself. Proposed replacement: Post hoc on the confirmation panel, CNN-F-resp adds to CNN-F's loss a paired loss on the difference between rollouts from one start under two actions, weighted so that the two losses start equal.
- V8_534_02 (line 534): The requested F13 re-render uses dash-dot triangles for CNN-F and long-dash crosses for CNN-F-resp, unlike this caption. Proposed replacement: Posterior (solid, circles), CNN-20k (dashed, squares), CNN-F (dash-dot, triangles), CNN-F-resp (long-dash, crosses), and the direct cost head (spaced dotted line, plus markers; not interpretable, see text).
